You can perform tasks such as: 'process_discovery' to extract a process model (e.g., Petri Net) from an event log, 'conformance_checking' to verify if the observed executions in the log match a given process model, 'performance_analysis' to compute performance indicators such as throughput time or activity frequencies, and 'filter_by_time_range' to filter the log between a specific time range. Given a natural language question, generate a JSON object containing one of these allowed 'task'. If the user question does not match an allowed task, kindly refuse to answer.Examples:
1. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you discover the process model from the production log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "process_discovery"}
2. Input (Natural Language Query): "The registered behavior in the event log is conforming to the factory model?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "conformance_checking"}
3. Input (Natural Language Query): "What is the mean throughput time of the produced pieces in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "throughput_time"}
4. Input (Natural Language Query): "What the frequencies of the activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "activity_frequency"}
5. Input (Natural Language Query): "What are the top 7 variants in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "top_variants", "k": 7}
6. Input (Natural Language Query): "Give me the resource analysis of the event log."
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "resource_analysis"}
7. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the start and end activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "start_end_activities"}
8. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the filtered event log between the start date 2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000 to the end date 2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "filter_by_time_range", "start_date": "2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000", "end_date": "2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000"}
 The only metrics allowed in the "performance_analysis" task are: "throughput_time", "activity_frequency", "top_variants", "start_end_activities", "resource_analysis".

You can perform tasks such as: 'process_discovery' to extract a process model (e.g., Petri Net) from an event log, 'conformance_checking' to verify if the observed executions in the log match a given process model, 'performance_analysis' to compute performance indicators such as throughput time or activity frequencies, and 'filter_by_time_range' to filter the log between a specific time range. Given a natural language question, generate a JSON object containing one of these allowed 'task'. If the user question does not match an allowed task, kindly refuse to answer.Examples:
1. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you discover the process model from the production log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "process_discovery"}
2. Input (Natural Language Query): "The registered behavior in the event log is conforming to the factory model?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "conformance_checking"}
3. Input (Natural Language Query): "What is the mean throughput time of the produced pieces in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "throughput_time"}
4. Input (Natural Language Query): "What the frequencies of the activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "activity_frequency"}
5. Input (Natural Language Query): "What are the top 7 variants in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "top_variants", "k": 7}
6. Input (Natural Language Query): "Give me the resource analysis of the event log."
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "resource_analysis"}
7. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the start and end activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "start_end_activities"}
8. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the filtered event log between the start date 2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000 to the end date 2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "filter_by_time_range", "start_date": "2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000", "end_date": "2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000"}
 The only metrics allowed in the "performance_analysis" task are: "throughput_time", "activity_frequency", "top_variants", "start_end_activities", "resource_analysis".
<|eot_id|><|start_header_id|>user<|end_header_id|>
Here is the context: 
Here is the user question: Can you list the start and end activities for the process?
<|eot_id|><|start_header_id|>assistant<|end_header_id|>
Model Answer: {
  "task": "start_and_end_activities",
  "metric": "start_end_activities",
  "start_date": "2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000",
  "end_date": "2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000"
}
Expected Answer: {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "start_end_activities"}
Verification Result: False

 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "throughput_time"}
4. Input (Natural Language Query): "What the frequencies of the activities in the event log?"

5. Input (Natural Language Query): "What are the top 7 variants in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "top_variants", "k": 7}
6. Input (Natural Language Query): "Give me the resource analysis of the event log."
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "resource_analysis"}
7. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the start and end activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "start_end_activities"}
8. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the filtered event log between the start date 2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000 to the end date 2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000?"

You can perform tasks such as: 'process_discovery' to extract a process model (e.g., Petri Net) from an event log, 'conformance_checking' to verify if the observed executions in the log match a given process model, 'performance_analysis' to compute performance indicators such as throughput time or activity frequencies, and 'filter_by_time_range' to filter the log between a specific time range. Given a natural language question, generate a JSON object containing one of these allowed 'task'. If the user question does not match an allowed task, kindly refuse to answer.Examples:
1. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you discover the process model from the production log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "process_discovery"}
2. Input (Natural Language Query): "The registered behavior in the event log is conforming to the factory model?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "conformance_checking"}
3. Input (Natural Language Query): "What is the mean throughput time of the produced pieces in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "throughput_time"}
4. Input (Natural Language Query): "What the frequencies of the activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "activity_frequency"}
5. Input (Natural Language Query): "What are the top 7 variants in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "top_variants", "k": 7}
6. Input (Natural Language Query): "Give me the resource analysis of the event log."
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "resource_analysis"}
7. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the start and end activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "start_end_activities"}
8. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the filtered event log between the start date 2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000 to the end date 2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "filter_by_time_range", "start_date": "2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000", "end_date": "2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000"}
 The only metrics allowed in the "performance_analysis" task are: "throughput_time", "activity_frequency", "top_variants", "start_end_activities", "resource_analysis".
<|eot_id|><|start_header_id|>user<|end_header_id|>
Here is the context: 
Here is the user question: Can you show the discovered Petri net model?
<|eot_id|><|start_header_id|>assistant<|end_header_id|>
Model Answer: {"task": "invalid_json_parse_error"}
Expected Answer: {"task": "process_discovery"}
Verification Result: False

Given a natural language question, generate a JSON object containing one of these allowed 'task'. If the user question does not match an allowed task, kindly refuse to answer.Examples:
1. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you discover the process model from the production log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "process_discovery"}
2. Input (Natural Language Query): "The registered behavior in the event log is conforming to the factory model?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "conformance_checking"}
3. Input (Natural Language Query): "What is the mean throughput time of the produced pieces in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "throughput_time"}
4. Input (Natural Language Query): "What the frequencies of the activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "activity_frequency"}
5. Input (Natural Language Query): "What are the top 7 variants in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "top_variants", "k": 7}
6. Input (Natural Language Query): "Give me the resource analysis of the event log."
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "resource_analysis"}
7. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the start and end activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "start_end_activities"}
8. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the filtered event log between the start date 2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000 to the end date 2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "filter_by_time_range", "start_date": "2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000", "end_date": "2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000"}
 The only metrics allowed in the "performance_analysis" task are: "throughput_time", "activity_frequency", "top_variants", "start_end_activities", "resource_analysis".
<|eot_id|><|start_header_id|>user<|end_header_id|>
Here is the context: 
Here is the user question: Can you discover the process model from the event log?
<|eot_id|><|start_header_id|>assistant<|end_header_id|>
Model Answer: {
  "task": "process_discovery",
  "metric": null,
  "allowed_tasks": [
    "process_discovery"
  ]
}
Expected Answer: {"task": "process_discovery"}
Verification Result: True

Given a natural language question, generate a JSON object containing one of these allowed 'task'. If the user question does not match an allowed task, kindly refuse to answer.Examples:
1. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you discover the process model from the production log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "process_discovery"}
2. Input (Natural Language Query): "The registered behavior in the event log is conforming to the factory model?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "conformance_checking"}
3. Input (Natural Language Query): "What is the mean throughput time of the produced pieces in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "throughput_time"}
4. Input (Natural Language Query): "What the frequencies of the activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "activity_frequency"}
5. Input (Natural Language Query): "What are the top 7 variants in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "top_variants", "k": 7}
6. Input (Natural Language Query): "Give me the resource analysis of the event log."
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "resource_analysis"}
7. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the start and end activities in the event log?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "performance_analysis", "metric": "start_end_activities"}
8. Input (Natural Language Query): "Can you provide me the filtered event log between the start date 2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000 to the end date 2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000?"
 Output: (LLM answer): {"task": "filter_by_time_range", "start_date": "2025-02-17T17:07:00.000000", "end_date": "2025-02-18T17:07:00.000000"}
 The only metrics allowed in the "performance_analysis" task are: "throughput_time", "activity_frequency", "top_variants", "start_end_activities", "resource_analysis".
<|eot_id|><|start_header_id|>user<|end_header_id|>
Here is the context: 
Here is the user question: Can you perform conformance checking between the event log and the reference model?
<|eot_id|><|start_header_id|>assistant<|end_header_id|>
Model Answer: {"task": "conformance_checking"}
Expected Answer: {"task": "conformance_checking"}
Verification Result: True
